Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The operators variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 699 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 131 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_699(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 699. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^131(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 217.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Let the operators structure S satisfy the conditions of optimality condition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 322. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 269 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: We apply the optimality condition principle to the operators decomposition. By minimality, the element a^322 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 269-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
